## Iteration 4 ƒ?" Code / trade route

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen scope remains exactly: `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutions.
- No permitted DB evidence is available yet; `db/CODEX/` is empty. Therefore, source code establishes possible/current routes, not historic proof for any trade.

- The current live entry route is:

  `CandleClosedAnalysisWorker` ƒ+' persisted live `TradeSignal` ƒ+' `PaperTradingService.processSignal` ƒ+' `SignalProcessingCoordinator` lock/freshness transaction ƒ+' execution-policy evaluation ƒ+' fresh canonical execution price ƒ+' wallet BUY ƒ+' `paper_position` insert.  
  See [CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.java:167), [SignalProcessingCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:64), and [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340).

- A BUY is rejected when no fresh canonical price is available, when the current execution price is at/below the stop or at/above the target, or when final entry-quality revalidation vetoes it. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1150)

- The prior log evidence for trade **182** names signal `164053` and entry code `BREAKOUT_CONTINUATION_ENTRY`. In current source, that route requires a recent ATR-deferred bullish 1m signal plus fresh non-bearish 5m/1h support, minimum score/confidence/evidence conditions, reward-risk of at least 1:1, and reduced sizing. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1740) This is route evidence only until DB evidence confirms that signal `164053` is linked to position 182 and records its contemporaneous inputs.

- Current mechanical-protection routing is:

  canonical price receipt ƒ+' freshness/order validation ƒ+' open managed-position lock ƒ+' TP ƒ+' absolute stop loss ƒ+' profit-lock ƒ+' normal multi-timeframe exit ƒ+' near-TP protection.  
  Stop loss has priority over all later exit policies. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173)

- For a stop loss, current code writes the wallet mechanical exit first, then closes the matching open `paper_position` by symbol, sets `close_reason=STOP_LOSS`, and records a production exit audit in the same transaction. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:175) [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391)

- For direct local price events, a committed `APPLIED` outcome is written transactionally after successful protection callback completion; a failed/rolled-back effect does not become `APPLIED`. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:80) This supportsƒ?"but does not by itself proveƒ?"the prior log-based inference that event `1837489` belonged to a committed protection route for trade 182.

- The prior log evidence for **182 / ICPUSDT** remains consistent with the current stop-loss route: observed price `3.465000000000` was below logged stop `3.465026371152`, followed by `Live STOP_LOSS executed` and `APPLIED` event `1837489`. Historic version/configuration and DB linkage remain unproven.

- Material selection-versus-route discrepancy: the charter says selected rows require `paper_position.status=CLOSED`, while the current stop-loss route writes `PaperPosition.status=STOPPED`; the enum distinguishes `CLOSED` and `STOPPED`. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:178) [PositionStatus.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PositionStatus.java:2)  
  Thus, if the current code applied at trade 182ƒ?Ts exit and the prior log linkage is correct, a direct stop-loss close would not meet the stated `status=CLOSED` selection predicate. This is not yet a confirmed defect: it may reflect a historical code version, later status normalization, an incorrect/misrecorded predicate, or an unconfirmed log-to-row association.

- Current normal protection selects the newest 1m/5m/1h signals without bounding them to the Binance observation timestamp. Only the near-TP route explicitly corrects future-dated signal lookup. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:95) [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:336)  
  This is a code-review observation, not a demonstrated cause of any frozen loss. Event and signal timestamps are required to determine whether it affected a trade.

- Paper wallet BUY and mechanical SELL records currently use zero fees, and `paper_position` P&L is calculated from entry/exit price and quantity. Any study conclusion must therefore call it paper/gross P&L unless independent fee evidence exists. [WalletAutoExecutionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:163) [WalletAutoExecutionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:533)

## Iteration 6 ƒ?" Root cause + research

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen scope remains exactly: `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. The permitted `db/CODEX/` folder contains **0 files**, so no DB proof-query result is available.
- **Trade 182 only:** prior position-specific log evidence supports a provisional mechanical stop-loss sequence for `ICPUSDT`:
  - Entry signal `164053` used `BREAKOUT_CONTINUATION_ENTRY`.
  - Entry completed at UTC `2026-10-11 22:41:04.863` / KSA `2026-10-12 01:41:04.863`.
  - A price observation at UTC `2026-10-11 22:49:33.460` / KSA `2026-10-12 01:49:33.460` was recorded `APPLIED` as event `1837489`.
  - Stop execution logged at UTC `2026-10-11 22:49:36.971` / KSA `2026-10-12 01:49:36.971`, at `3.465000000000` versus stop `3.465026371152`.
- Current source makes stop loss absolute: at or below the managed stop, it performs the mechanical exit and records `STOP_LOSS`. The source also shows execution-time revalidation against a fresh price and a no-chase guard. This is current-route evidence only, not proof of the historic deployed version.
- Trade 182ƒ?Ts logged static geometry was reward/risk ƒ%^ **1.589**, above the current continuation routeƒ?Ts 1:1 minimum. That supports neither full entry quality nor the eventual lossƒ?Ts cause.
- The code-path discrepancy remains material: current stop-loss routing saves `PositionStatus.STOPPED`, while the charter selection predicate is `status=CLOSED`. Without the actual row, this is unresolvedƒ?"not a confirmed selection error or code defect.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- **Provisional root cause for 182:** adverse price movement after a continuation entry reached the configured stop; available evidence supports a completed mechanical protection exit, not a missed-stop failure.
- The approximately **3.514 s** from recorded observation to logged stop execution, including a **2.438 s** symbol transaction, is a measurement concern. It does not establish that latency caused or worsened the loss: the accepted observation was already below the stop, and `APPLIED` evidence follows.
- Root cause for **172, 162, 158, 143, and 137 is unestablished**. Classifying them as strategy losses, stale-data losses, execution defects, or protection failures would be unsupported.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- Binance documents that spot kline streams include an explicit closed-candle flag and that non-1-second kline updates are delivered every two seconds; it separately offers real-time trade streams. This means a kline-derived observation must not be treated as a complete exchange-tick record. It does **not** prove any missed move, delay, or defect in these six trades. [Binance WebSocket Streams](https://developers.binance.com/zh-CN/docs/products/spot/testnet/web-socket-streams)

### UNKNOWNS

- All durable attributes and links for the six authoritative rows: symbol, status, entry/exit, quantity, P&L, close reason, linked signal/work/wallet/managed-position/price-event/audit IDs.
- Whether logged signal `164053` and price event `1837489` durably link to position 182.
- Whether the 3.514-second path changed the paper exit price relative to a later/earlier accepted observation.
- Runtime version, ingestion mode, configuration, data freshness, BTC/HTF context, and Replay reproducibility for every frozen trade.
- Snapshot metadata remains inconsistent: the supplied UTC timestamp converts to KSA `2026-10-22 13:28:18`, not the separately supplied KSA timestamp.

## Iteration 7 ƒ?" Self-challenge

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen scope remains exactly: `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutions.
- `db/CODEX/` still has no result files. Therefore no durable DB evidence supports any final per-trade classification.

- Trade **182** has stronger log linkage than ƒ?opossible,ƒ?? though it remains unconfirmed by the authoritative row:
  - Signal `164053` for `ICPUSDT` was processed at UTC `2026-10-11 22:41:03.002` / KSA `2026-10-12 01:41:03.002`.
  - The entry route allowed `BREAKOUT_CONTINUATION_ENTRY` at UTC `22:41:03.047` and passed its price recheck at `22:41:03.060`.
  - Its symbol transaction completed `COMMITTED` at UTC `22:41:04.863`; the same flow logged `paperPositionOpened=true`.
  - At UTC `22:41:05.405`, the next ICP protection evaluation explicitly identifies `positionId=182`, entry `3.493`, target `3.537460443272`, and an inactive profit lock.
  - This makes the `164053` ƒ+' position 182 association strongly supported by runtime ordering, but not DB-proven.

- The stop sequence for **182** is similarly well-supported, but not a durable financial reconciliation:
  - Event `1837489` was reported `APPLIED` for `ICPUSDT`, observed UTC `2026-10-11 22:49:33.460` / KSA `2026-10-12 01:49:33.460`.
  - The application logged `Live STOP_LOSS executed` at UTC `22:49:36.971` / KSA `01:49:36.971`, with price `3.465000000000` versus stop `3.465026371152`.
  - The symbol transaction then reported `COMMITTED` after `2,438 ms`; event `1837489` was reported `APPLIED` at UTC `22:49:36.974`.
  - The runtime evidence supports a completed stop-loss path. It does **not** prove the final `paper_position` row, P&L, wallet execution, or whether the recorded price was worsened by the delay.

- The prior conclusion that the ~3.514-second observation-to-completion interval is not yet a demonstrated defect remains correct. The first observed event was already in the stop path, but the eventƒ?Ts stored price and intervening executable-price path are unavailable. Causation cannot be inferred.

- A material **selection-consistency challenge** remains:
  - The charter fixes the selection predicate as `paper_position.status=CLOSED`.
  - Current source sets a mechanical stop-loss close to `PositionStatus.STOPPED`; the enum distinguishes `CLOSED` and `STOPPED`.
  - Git blame dates that `STOPPED` stop-loss assignment to 2026-08-13, before the 2026-10-11 runtime event.
  - No source path was found that converts an already `STOPPED` paper position to `CLOSED`; manual close also rejects non-`OPEN` rows.
  - Thus, if the logged stop is position 182 and the running binary followed this source lineage, **182 should not satisfy the supplied `status=CLOSED` predicate**. This is an unresolved evidence contradictionƒ?"not a confirmed selection errorƒ?"until the exact row and executed selection proof are supplied.

- Trades **172, 162, 158, 143, and 137** remain unclassified. No position-specific logs or DB records were available.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- Trade 182 is best described as a **probable valid mechanical stop-loss**, not a confirmed protection failure. The conclusion remains provisional because the authoritative position, wallet, audit, and price-event records are absent.
- It would be circular to treat the charterƒ?Ts selection predicate as proof that 182 could not have been stopped; the exact row is the evidence needed to resolve that contradiction.
- No inference about the other five losses is justified.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- None in this iteration.

- Runtime log evidence: `iteration03-codex.stderr.txt`, archived-log excerpts for signal `164053`, position `182`, and event `1837489`.

  - `LivePositionProtectionService.java:173ƒ?"180` performs mechanical stop-loss handling and passes `PositionStatus.STOPPED`.

1151:         // FIX-122: recheck only stamped initial approvals; ADD approvals carry no stamp.
1152:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) approvedDecision = stopLossEvidence.recheck(signal, approvedDecision);
1153:         if (approvedDecision != null && !approvedDecision.allowed()) return approvedDecision;

1157:                     "A fresh positive execution price is required before wallet execution.");

1166:         }

1168:         return applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(approvedDecision, quality).withStopBoundary(approvedDecision.stopBoundary());

```sql
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'market_price_event',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'position_management_event',
      'signal_processing_work'
  )
ORDER BY c.table_name, c.ordinal_position;

SELECT p.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position AS p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);

SELECT p.id, p.status, p.closed_at, p.realized_pnl
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position AS p
WHERE p.status = 'CLOSED'
  AND p.closed_at IS NOT NULL
  AND p.realized_pnl < 0
ORDER BY p.closed_at DESC
LIMIT 6;
```

### END_DB_REQUEST

### AGENT INFERENCE

Position 182 is best described as a probable mechanically completed stop loss, not a demonstrated missed-stop or execution defect. This remains provisional because log order is not a durable financial linkage.

No position-specific log evidence supports a classification for IDs 172, 162, 158, 143, or 137.

## CODE EVIDENCE

### PROJECT EVIDENCE

Current source shows:

- A BUY resolves a fresh canonical execution price and revalidates it; entries at/below stop or at/above target are rejected. [`PaperTradingService.java`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340), [`ExecutionIntelligenceService.java`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1150)
- The continuation route requires non-bearish 5m/1h context and reward/risk of at least 1:1. [`ExecutionIntelligenceService.java`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1740)
- Stop loss is absolute; successful mechanical exit calls `closePaper(..., STOPPED, "STOP_LOSS", ...)`. [`LivePositionProtectionService.java`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173)
- `closePaper` persists exit price, realized P&L, status, reason, and close time. [`LivePositionProtectionService.java`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391)
- A direct price event reaches `APPLIED` only within an active effects transaction and logs the outcome after commit. [`MarketPriceEventService.java`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:80)

Current source distinguishes `CLOSED` from `STOPPED`. This conflicts with the supplied frozen selection only if position 182’s logged stop is durably linked to that row and the historical binary followed this source lineage. It is an unresolved contradiction, not a confirmed defect.
